Conclusion: 成功分离了$f(R)$引力中标量子局部稳定性与视界输运动力学，建立了输运性质与标量子质量判据的独立性框架。固定背景估计表明标量子尾部可能影响内视界广义熵演化，但该估计未确立非线性质量膨胀或特定可延拓性类别。研究为$f(R)$引力的耦合规律性、背景输运、标量子稳定性和内视界非线性演化提供了分层理论框架。

Abstract: The Dolgov-Kawasaki instability tests the local scalaron response in metric $f(R)$ gravity, but does not determine strong-field horizon dynamics. We separate these questions using $F=f_R$ and the Legendre potential $\mathcal U(F)=FR-f$. The scalaron restoring coefficient is $m_{\rm sc}^2=(F-Rf_{RR})/(3f_{RR})$, whereas exact spherical Wald-area transport identities cancel the scalaron Hessian. Their Einstein-frame interpretation distinguishes longitudinal kinetic focusing from potential-dependent cross-focusing. We prove that, at fixed constant-curvature background with tracefree matter, transport depends only on $(f,f_R)$, while the scalaron mass depends on the independently adjustable $f_{RR}$. Reissner-Nordström in $f(R)=R+αR^2$ gives an exact discriminator: identical background transport coexists with opposite scalaron mass signs. A charged solution with varying $F$ and a cosmological check test the identities beyond this constant-$F$ sector. For a nonzero massive scalaron tail transmitted to a nondegenerate Cauchy horizon, a conditional fixed-background estimate gives a nonintegrable contribution to generalized entropy focusing. This estimate does not establish nonlinear mass inflation or a particular extendibility class. The results provide a layered framework for coupling regularity, background transport, scalaron stability, and nonlinear inner-horizon evolution.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a novel systematic method for constructing exact, static, and asymptotically flat black-hole solutions in shift-symmetric and $Z_2$-symmetric beyond Horndeski theories. In contrast to previous studies, our approach does not rely on the restrictive reciprocal condition $g_{tt}g_{rr}=-1$, allowing for more general geometries characterized by two independent metric functions. The resulting solution space contains two qualitatively distinct types of hair originating from different branches of the theory. One branch is characterized by khronometric hair, while the other by genuine scalar hair. In both, the scalar profile defines a nontrivial preferred foliation, but the shift-symmetry Noether current and the associated propagating degree of freedom are trivial for the former and nonvanishing for the latter. Focusing mainly on the second branch, we derive several distinct families of exact black-hole solutions. In general, these solutions are singular and exhibit primary scalar hair associated with an independent scalar charge. Nevertheless, we demonstrate that they can be regularized to yield regular black-hole or solitonic configurations, for which the scalar hair becomes secondary. We further investigate the stability properties of these solutions under linear perturbations by analyzing the axial sector and the monopole $(\ell=0)$ mode of the even-parity sector. For representative solutions with scalar hair, the perturbative analysis provides evidence for linear stability under the modes considered.

Abstract: Extra spatial dimensions arise naturally in theories beyond four-dimensional general relativity (GR), but direct observational evidence remains elusive. Gravitational waves offer a distinctive probe: geometric projection onto the observable three-dimensional spatial subspace can produce apparent tensor, vector, and scalar polarizations. We consider one additional spatial dimension and a two-mode tensor-wave ansatz for which the projected polarization components are fixed linear combinations of the same two underlying GR tensor waveforms. These components enter the measured strain through the standard detector antenna patterns. A frequency-domain Bayesian analysis of five compact-binary events constrains two rotation angles for two noncommuting orderings of rotations that preserve propagation within the observed subspace. Two events are further analyzed for four orderings that allow propagation to tilt into the extra dimension. For GW250114, the maximum GR quantiles among the tested rotation orderings are $Q_{\mathrm{GR}}\simeq0.6\%$ for the two-angle fits and $Q^{\mathrm{marg}}_{\mathrm{GR}}\simeq4.6\%$ for the three-angle fits after marginalizing over the third angle. Across the full event sample, the GR reference point lies inside the estimated 90% highest-posterior-density region in every case.

Abstract: We investigate the redistribution and directional properties of Gaussian tripartite quantum steering in de-Sitter space within the continuous-variable framework. By expressing the Bunch-Davies vacuum in terms of the open-chart modes through a Bogoliubov transformation, we derive the covariance matrix of the resulting multipartite Gaussian state and analyze both one-to-two and two-to-one steering configurations. We find that de-Sitter curvature generally suppresses the steering initially shared among the accessible modes, but its influence depends strongly on the steering direction. In particular, while some steering configurations undergo sudden death at finite curvature, two-to-one steering toward a nongravitational mode remains finite throughout the parameter range considered, demonstrating a robustness of tripartite steering absent in its bipartite counterpart. Remarkably, the maximum steering asymmetry occurs at the critical point at which steering in one direction undergoes sudden death, marking the transition from two-way to one-way steering. We further show that the de-Sitter gravitational field can generate tripartite steering involving modes separated by the cosmological horizon. Depending on the steering partition, this curvature-induced steering exhibits persistent one-way or asymmetric two-way behavior, as well as a sudden-birth transition from one-way to two-way steering. These results demonstrate that de-Sitter curvature not only degrades preexisting quantum correlations but also redistributes and generates directional multipartite steering across causally disconnected regions.

</details>

Conclusion: 双星微引力透镜事件结合高精度干涉天体测量技术为在极弱引力场条件下检验广义相对论提供了可行的新方法。GRAVITY+仪器的部署使得对暗弱且角间距极小的双星透镜进行天体测量成为可能。当前方法可实现约23%精度的PPN参数γ测量,随着更多高质量观测数据的积累,未来有望将检验精度提升至3%,为理解引力在极端弱场条件下的行为提供重要约束。

Abstract: Einstein's General Relativity (GR), which has been precisely tested on galaxy and solar system scales, is still poorly constrained in extremely weak gravitational fields (with dimensionless Newtonian potentials of $Φ\sim 10^{-8}$). In this Letter, we propose a new method to probe such a weak-field regime of gravity and measure the spatial curvature generated per unit mass. Specifically, binary microlensing events provide a laboratory to measure the parameterized post-Newtonian (PPN) parameter ($γ$), by combining the lensing mass ($M_{\rm lens}^{\rm GR}$) inferred from microlensing with the dynamical mass ($M_{\rm dyn}$) determined from interferometric astrometry. Astrometric tracking of binary lenses was previously hindered by their faintness and minuscule angular separations (typically a few milliarcseconds). This limitation has been alleviated by the deployment of GRAVITY+, which pushes the instrument's sensitivity and astrometric precision to unprecedented heights. Based on astrometric simulations of the binary microlensing event ASASSN-22av, we directly estimate $γ$ at the precision of ~23%. Such test of GR in the extremely weak-field regime can be further improved to much higher precision (~3%), based on more binary microlensing events with precise determination of the dynamical/lens mass.

</details>


### [98] [Gravitational radiation from a photon on the light ring: multipole fluxes and the logarithmic divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12082)
*Enrico Barausse*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild和Kerr黑洞的圆形零测地线上无质量粒子辐射引力波的问题,发现辐射功率随多极矩$\ell$仅以$1/\ell$衰减,导致总瞬时功率呈对数发散。这一现象源于光子与准正则模式的近共振效应以及无质量粒子源项的特殊抵消机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑洞物理中,粒子沿测地线运动时的引力波辐射是理解黑洞动力学和引力波信号的关键。特别是光环(light ring)附近的无质量粒子辐射行为尚未被完全理解。以往对大质量粒子的研究存在理论缺陷(如奇宇称项计算错误),需要系统性地从第一性原理推导无质量粒子在Schwarzschild和Kerr黑洞光环上的引力波辐射特性,并理解其多极展开的渐近行为及物理机制。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与数值验证相结合的方法:1)从Zerilli-Regge-Wheeler方程和Teukolsky方程出发,以零应力-能量张量为源项进行解析推导;2)分析多极辐射功率$\dot{E}_\ell$的渐近行为,推导出通用公式$\dot{E}_\ell\to g(a) κE^2/(M^2\ell)$,其中$κ=0.063653$,自旋因子$g(a)=27M^2(r_0-M)/[r_0^2(r_0+3M)]$;3)探究$1/\ell$行为的两个物理原因:光子与准正则模式的近共振抑制了大$\ell$时的指数衰减,以及无质量粒子源项中的特殊抵消使功率降低$\ell^2$因子;4)计算辐射在无穷远和黑洞视界的分配比例及修正项;5)扩展到光环附近的大质量粒子情形;6)通过数值求解验证理论结果(Schwarzschild情形至$\ell=12800$,Kerr情形至$\ell=800$)。

Result: 主要结果包括:1)证明了无质量粒子在黑洞光环上的引力波辐射功率按$1/\ell$衰减,总功率对数发散;2)获得了适用于任意自旋的解析公式,包括精确的数值系数$κ=0.063653$和闭合形式的自旋因子$g(a)$;3)揭示了$1/\ell$行为的双重物理机制:准正则模式近共振和源项抵消效应;4)在主导阶,辐射平均分配到无穷远和黑洞视界,并计算了非旋转情形下的首阶修正;5)发现Breuer等人(1973)关于大质量粒子测地同步辐射的奇宇称项存在4倍误差,本文给出了正确结果;6)数值计算验证了理论预言在极高多极矩下的准确性;7)阐明了质量如何将$1/\ell$律转变为指数截断。

Conclusion: 本文系统解决了无质量粒子在黑洞光环上引力波辐射的理论问题,揭示了其独特的$1/\ell$多极衰减规律及对数发散特性,并阐明了其物理起源。研究纠正了文献中关于大质量粒子测地同步辐射的历史错误,为理解极端质量比旋近系统、黑洞阴影附近的引力波信号以及引力波天文学提供了重要的理论基础。该工作展示了黑洞准正则模式与粒子辐射之间的深刻联系,对引力波数据分析和黑洞物理的精密检验具有重要意义。

Abstract: A massless particle on the circular null geodesic of a Schwarzschild or Kerr black hole radiates gravitational waves at all multipoles $\ell$ with a power that falls off only as $1/\ell$, so that the total instantaneous power is logarithmically divergent. We derive this result analytically from the Zerilli--Regge--Wheeler and Teukolsky equations with a null stress-energy tensor as source, and find that both the power radiated to infinity and the power absorbed by the horizon approach $\dot{E}_\ell\to g(a) κE^2/(M^2\ell)$, where $E$ is the energy of the particle, $M$ the mass of the black hole and $a$ its spin, with $κ=0.063653$ and a spin factor $g(a)$, for which we obtain, for any spin, the closed form $g=27M^2(r_0-M)/[r_0^2(r_0+3M)]$ in terms of the radius $r_0$ of the Kerr light ring. The $1/\ell$ behavior has two causes: the near-resonance of the photon with the quasinormal modes, which prevents any exponential suppression at large $\ell$, and a cancellation in the source that is specific to massless particles, which reduces the power per multipole by a factor $\ell^2$ with respect to a generic source. At leading order half of the radiation falls into the black hole, and we compute the first correction to this equal split in the non-spinning case. The same analysis describes massive particles on orbits close to the light ring, where the mass turns the $1/\ell$ law into an exponential cut-off. We recover the dominant, even-parity term of the geodesic synchrotron radiation formulae for massive particles of Breuer, Ruffini, Tiomno and Vishveshwara (1973), but we find that their odd-parity term is too small by a factor of four. All results are checked against numerical solutions, up to $\ell=12800$ in Schwarzschild and $\ell=800$ in Kerr.

</details>

Abstract: We classify all diffeomorphism-invariant theories of gravity which possess traced field equations of motion of second order in derivatives. We also prove in the affirmative a conjecture regarding the form of theories constructed algebraically from the Riemann tensor with second-order traced field equations and classify all polynomial quasi-topological gravities with second-order traced field equations.

</details>


### [100] [The variance of the CMB temperature gradient: a dependence on the spatial orientation and anisotropy of a multiply connected Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12179)

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种模型独立的方法来揭示宇宙微波背景(CMB)温度图中的各向异性和几何模式,通过ρ统计量和掩模扫描技术,检测到与3-环面拓扑宇宙相符的周期性几何结构,并确定了宇宙有限尺度约为3个哈勃长度。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在具有环面拓扑的宇宙模型中成功检测到与预期"天空中的圆圈"几何匹配的微弱但持续的几何和周期性结构。重新验证了立方环面尺度L与ρ统计量之间的线性关系,确定宇宙有限尺度s约为3个哈勃长度。发现统计各向同性的违背非常微弱,几乎不影响ρ与L之间的线性关系。证明了ρ不仅是宇宙空间有限性和尺度的统计特征,还能通过掩模扫描过程勾勒出CMB的各向异性模式。

Conclusion: ρ统计量结合掩模扫描方法是揭示CMB各向异性几何模式的有效工具,能够检测宇宙拓扑结构(如立方环面拓扑)的特征信号。研究支持宇宙可能具有多重连通的有限拓扑结构,尺度约为3个哈勃长度,且这种拓扑施加的周期性边界条件约束了宇宙的整体不均匀性程度并要求零平均曲率。该方法为探测宇宙的全局拓扑性质提供了新的途径。

Abstract: In the present work we develop a model--independent method to reveal the underlying patterns of anisotropy and geometry in CMB temperature maps. This method, based on the rho-statistics and a process of mask--sweeping, unveils faint but persistent geometrical and periodic structures characterising the anisotropy of the CMB in a universe with 3-torus topology. We assess that the patterns detected over large ensembles of toroidal maps match geometrically to the layouts of expected circles-in-the-sky. The mask--sweeping method explores the whole CMB over 768 mask orientations. Thus we are able to re-verify the linear relation between L the size of the cubic torus and rho the standard deviation of the normalised variance of the CMB temperature gradient. This confers a finite size s of approximately 3 Hubble lengths to our Universe with toroidal topology. Furthermore, it is shown that the violation of statistical isotropy is so faint that this has almost no influence on the linear relation between rho and L. A topology like the cubic toroidal one imposes to our Universe periodic conditions (that can be seen as periodic boundary conditions) which constrain the global degree of inhomogeneity and a zero average curvature. We demonstrate here that rho is not only a statistical signature of the spatial finiteness and size of our Universe (may its topology be simply or multiply connected) but also outlines the patterns of anisotropy of the CMB with the help of the mask--sweeping process.

</details>


### [101] [Swirling spacetimes in higher dimensions: Vacuum backgrounds and black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12372)
*José Barrientos,Adolfo Cisterna,Carlos Herdeiro,Konstantinos Pallikaris*

TL;DR: 本文利用降维理论的隐藏对称性在真空广义相对论中构造了高维旋涡时空，在五维中沿两个独立轴向Killing矢量进行约化，获得了具有两个独立旋涡参数的SL(3,ℝ)/SO(3) sigma模型和真正的五维背景，发现了新的多向渐近结构和方向性曲率奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旋涡时空构造主要集中在低维情况，缺乏对高维旋涡时空的系统研究。本文旨在利用降维理论的隐藏对称性，构造具有多个独立旋涡参数的高维真空时空解，并探索其独特的渐近性质和奇点结构，从而深化对广义相对论中高维旋转时空几何的理解。

Method: 采用协变空间(coset-space)表述方法，在五维时空中沿两个独立的轴向Killing矢量进行维度约化，将系统约化为三维的SL(3,ℝ)/SO(3) sigma模型。通过该约化变换构造具有两个独立旋涡参数的五维背景时空，并将同样的变换应用于Schwarzschild-Tangherlini和Myers-Perry几何以获得双旋涡解。进一步分析时空的渐近结构，采用关联的径向-角向极限揭示方向性奇点，并通过测地线分析研究奇异渐近端的因果结构。最后系统研究该构造向更高维度的最大轴向扩展。

Result: 成功构造了具有两个独立旋涡参数的真正五维背景时空，并获得了双旋涡的Schwarzschild-Tangherlini和Myers-Perry几何。发现新背景具有多向渐近结构这一显著特征，通过关联径向-角向极限揭示了在固定角度的常规大半径极限中不可见的方向性曲率奇点。证明存在因果测地线在有限仿射参数内到达这些奇异渐近端。确定了该构造的维度限制:五维是完全轴向约化到三维的最大维度,六维允许额外的非平凡平移形变,更高维度仅贡献平坦的旁观方向。

Conclusion: 本文通过降维理论的隐藏对称性成功构造了高维旋涡时空,揭示了其独特的多向渐近结构和方向性曲率奇点等新颖几何特征。五维是完全轴向约化的最大维度,六维及以上维度的扩展受到结构性限制。这些结果拓展了对广义相对论中高维旋转时空几何的认识,为研究更复杂的引力系统提供了新的理论工具和几何图像。方向性奇点的发现表明需要超越常规极限分析来全面理解高维时空的渐近行为。

Abstract: We construct higher-dimensional swirling spacetimes in vacuum General Relativity using the hidden symmetries of the dimensionally reduced theory. Working directly with the coset-space formulation, we perform in five dimensions a reduction along the two independent axial Killing vectors, obtaining an $SL(3,\mathbb{R})/SO(3)$ sigma model and a genuinely five-dimensional background with two independent swirling parameters. The same transformation yields doubly swirling Schwarzschild--Tangherlini and Myers--Perry geometries. A distinctive feature of the new background is its multidirectional asymptotic structure. Correlated radial-angular limits reveal directional curvature singularities that are invisible in the usual large-radius limit at fixed angle. We further exhibit causal geodesics that reach such singular asymptotic ends in finite affine parameter. Finally, we describe the maximally axial extension of this construction to higher dimensions: five dimensions are maximal for an entirely axial reduction to three dimensions, while six dimensions admit an additional nontrivial translational deformation, with further dimensions contributing only flat spectator directions.

</details>


### [102] [A Pairwise Surrogate for Gravitational-Wave Spectra from Highly Relativistic Vacuum Bubble Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12389)
*Malte Buschmann,Toby Opferkuch*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模拟的成对替代方法来预测真空一阶相变产生的引力波谱，通过将微观碰撞动力学与多气泡转变几何分离，利用低维模拟构建双气泡引力波谱库，并结合几何权重重构多气泡系统的引力波谱，大幅降低了计算成本并能够处理高度相对论性碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接晶格模拟预测真空一阶相变产生的引力波谱面临巨大挑战：需要在包含多个气泡的大体积中解析洛伦兹收缩的标量气泡壁，特别是在大推进（高相对论速度）regime下计算成本极其昂贵，甚至无法实现。因此需要开发一种计算高效且能够处理高度相对论性碰撞的替代方法。

Method: 开发了一种基于模拟的成对替代（pairwise surrogate）方法，核心思想是将微观碰撞动力学与多气泡转变的几何结构分离：(1) 利用对称性简化的(1+1)维标量场模拟构建双气泡引力波谱库，包含非线性碰撞后演化；(2) 引入几何权重因子来考虑其他气泡的屏蔽效应；(3) 将双气泡谱与几何权重结合，重构三气泡和多气泡系统的引力波谱；(4) 通过完整的(3+1)维晶格模拟对双气泡谱和重构谱进行验证。

Result: 替代方法在计算效率上比直接模拟提高了数个数量级，同时能够处理直接多气泡模拟无法达到的高度相对论性碰撞。通过与完整(3+1)维晶格模拟的对比验证，证实了双气泡谱和重构的三气泡及多气泡系统谱的准确性。该方法成功地为研究引力波谱的振幅和形状如何依赖于碰撞推进和标量势提供了可行途径。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了一种计算高效的替代方法来预测真空一阶相变的引力波谱，通过将问题分解为双气泡碰撞动力学和多气泡几何配置两部分，克服了直接晶格模拟在高相对论regime下的计算瓶颈。该方法不仅大幅降低了计算成本，还拓展了可研究的参数空间，为系统研究碰撞boost和标量势对引力波谱特征的影响开辟了新途径，对引力波天文学和早期宇宙相变研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from vacuum first-order phase transitions probe the nonlinear dynamics of relativistic bubble collisions. Predicting their spectrum requires resolving Lorentz-contracted scalar bubble walls throughout a volume containing many bubbles, making the large-boost regime prohibitively expensive for direct lattice simulations. We develop a simulation-based pairwise surrogate that separates the microscopic collision dynamics from the geometry of the multi-bubble transition. This utilizes symmetry-reduced $(1+1)$-dimensional scalar-field simulations to build a library of two-bubble gravitational-wave spectra, including the nonlinear post-collision evolution. These spectra are combined with geometric weights that account for screening by other bubbles. We test the two-bubble spectra and the reconstructed spectra of three- and many-bubble systems against full $(3+1)$-dimensional lattice simulations. Not only can the surrogate be orders of magnitude cheaper computationally, but it also provides access to highly relativistic collisions beyond the practical reach of direct many-bubble simulations. This provides a route to studying how the spectral amplitude and shape depend on the collision boost and scalar potential.

</details>


### [103] [Listening to the Horizon: Probing Near-Horizon Reflectivity with GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12429)
*R. Prasad,Subhodeep Sarkar,Sudipta Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用引力波事件GW250114中新识别的直接波(DW)成分，提出了一种检验黑洞视界反射性的新方法。通过搜索由奇异致密天体表面反射产生的延迟回波信号，对视界反射率进行了约束，在90%置信度下得到反射率上限|ℛₛ|<0.06，将直接波信号转化为对视界性质的直接检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波天文学的核心目标之一是检验天体物理黑洞的视界性质。新识别的直接波(DW)作为GW250114事件中一个非准正模的瞬时成分，为近视界物理提供了新的探测手段。尽管直接波本身是否携带视界信息尚不明确，但研究者希望利用它来检验视界反射性——如果并合残余不是标准黑洞而是具有部分反射表面的奇异致密天体(ECO)，则向内坠落的辐射会被反射并泄漏回来，形成可探测的延迟回波信号。

Method: 研究者构建了归一化的回波模板，该模板基于观测到的直接波信号，其振幅由表面反射率(|ℛₛ|)决定。具体方法包括：(1)建立奇异致密天体表面反射产生延迟直接波回波的理论模型；(2)用观测到的直接波对回波模板进行归一化处理；(3)在经过准正模滤波的数据中搜索该回波信号；(4)使用贝叶斯方法计算证据比并对表面反射率进行约束，考察范围从距假想视界0.1 Mf到普朗克长度的表面位置。

Result: 在准正模滤波后的数据中未发现回波信号的证据，贝叶斯因子为ln𝓑=-2.9。对于距假想视界0.1 Mf到普朗克长度范围内的表面，在90%置信度下得到表面反射率上限|ℛₛ|<0.06，对应的反射能量占比低于0.4%。这一结果表明在当前观测精度下，GW250114的并合残余与标准黑洞(无反射视界)的预期一致。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将引力波信号中的瞬时直接波成分转化为对黑洞视界反射性的直接检验工具。通过对GW250114事件的分析，在高置信度下排除了高反射率奇异致密天体的可能性，为并合残余具有吸收性视界提供了支持证据。这种基于直接波-回波关联的新方法为引力波天文学中检验黑洞视界性质开辟了新途径，对理解强引力场区域的物理本质具有重要意义。

Abstract: Testing the horizon properties of astrophysical black holes is a central goal of gravitational-wave astronomy. The recently identified Direct Wave (DW), a prompt non-quasinormal component of GW250114, provides a new probe of near-horizon physics. While it remains unclear whether the DW itself carries horizon information, we show that it enables a novel null test of horizon reflectivity. If the remnant is an exotic compact object (ECO) with a partially reflecting surface, inward plunge radiation is reflected and leaks back through the gravitational potential barrier, producing a delayed DW echo whose amplitude is set by the surface reflectivity ($|\mathcal{R}_s|$). We construct the corresponding echo template, normalized by the observed DW, and search for it in quasinormal-mode-filtered data. We find no evidence for an echo ($\ln\mathcal{B}=-2.9$) and obtain the 90\% credible bound $|\mathcal{R}_s|<0.06$, corresponding to a reflected energy fraction below $0.4\%$, for surfaces from $0.1\, M_f$ to a Planck length from the would-be horizon. Our method thus turns the prompt ringdown signal into a direct test of horizon reflectivity.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Closed Universe, Quantum Entanglement and Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10667)
*Hao Geng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究量子引力中"观察者"的出现问题,统一了三类不同的观察者模型,证明它们都可归于自发破缺微分同胚对称性产生的Goldstone玻色子描述,并将观察者的出现与Massive Islands范式联系起来,表明引力子质量是量子态的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力理论认为闭合宇宙的希尔伯特空间是平凡的一维空间,但现实宇宙却展现出丰富的结构。为解决这一矛盾,需要理解"观察者"如何使非平凡希尔伯特空间涌现。尽管已有三类不同的观察者模型,但它们之间的内在联系尚不清楚,需要建立统一的理论框架来理解观察者的本质。

Method: 本文采用理论分析方法,系统研究三类观察者模型:(1)哈密顿量中相空间变量的线性项;(2)量子电路全息模型中与外部非引力系统纠缠的子系统;(3)微分同胚自发破缺时涌现的Goldstone玻色子。通过理论推导,证明第二类模型可以统一到第三类模型中,并将观察者的出现与Massive Islands范式相联系,分析引力子质量作为量子态特征的物理意义。

Result: 成功证明了量子电路全息模型中的观察者(第二类)可以统一到微分同胚自发破缺的Goldstone玻色子框架(第三类)中。研究表明观察者的出现是Massive Islands范式的体现,引力子质量是量子态的特征而非经典参数。在蒸发黑洞的晚期阶段,应将island视为闭合宇宙,与引力子质量相关的Goldstone玻色子即为观察者。此外,发现观察者能够"观测"量子引力中的整体对称性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子引力中观察者模型的统一理论框架,证明不同类型的观察者模型本质上都可归结为微分同胚对称性自发破缺产生的Goldstone玻色子。观察者的出现与Massive Islands范式紧密相关,揭示了引力子质量作为量子态特征的深刻物理意义。这一结果不仅解决了闭合宇宙希尔伯特空间平凡性与实际宇宙丰富性之间的矛盾,还为理解量子引力中的观察者角色、island几何以及整体对称性提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: It has been argued that in quantum gravity the Hilbert space associated with any closed universe is trivially one-dimensional. To reconcile this argument with the richness of our own universe, it was suggested that a nontrivial Hilbert space will emerge once an ``observer" is included. Such an ``observer" has been modeled in different ways. There are three concrete classes of models: $\textbf{1)}$ The ``observer" is modeled by a term in the full Hamiltonian of the system which is linear in a phase space variable; $\textbf{2)}$ The ``observer" is modeled in the quantum circuit model of holography as a subsystem in the closed universe which is entangled with an external non-gravitational system; $\textbf{3)}$ The ``observer" is argued to be described by the emergent Goldstone bosons when the diffeomorphisms are spontaneously broken. In fact, the third class was discovered by an attempt to physically realize the first class. In this paper, we will show that the second class can also be unified into the third class. Our result indicates that the emergence of the ``observer" is a manifestation of the $\textit{Massive Islands}$ paradigm. It demonstrates that the graviton mass is a feature of the quantum state. One should think of the islands as closed universes and the Goldstone boson associated with the graviton mass as the ``observer", which is exactly the case at late-times for evaporating black holes. An implication of this work is that the ``observer" can ``observe" global symmetries in quantum gravity.

</details>


### [105] [Thermodynamics of interacting magnetic chain molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10671)
*Alexios P. Polychronakos,Konstantinos Sfetsos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究由任意数量磁性活性原子组成的链状分子磁体的热力学性质，发现系统呈现丰富的铁磁相变级联结构，并在存在杂质时出现边界态，长链极限下磁化态对应孤子解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解由多个磁性原子组成的链状分子磁体的热力学行为和相变特性，特别是在不同耦合强度和存在杂质情况下的复杂相结构，这对于设计和调控分子磁性材料具有重要意义。

Method: 采用平均场近似方法推导自旋-j SU(2)磁性原子链的热力学性质，确定平衡态和稳定性条件。针对长链情况引入连续近似描述，将磁化态映射为扭结孤子。分析了链端存在杂质（修正耦合）时的边界态效应。

Result: 系统展现出丰富的相结构，具有级联式铁磁相变，相变数量从m（最大铁磁态）到零（顺磁态）不等，取决于耦合强度的相对大小。当链端存在杂质时，边界态会出现并与顺磁相或完全铁磁相共存。在连续近似下，低于临界温度的磁化态对应于扭结孤子解。

Conclusion: 链状分子磁体呈现由耦合强度调控的多重铁磁相变级联现象，杂质可诱导边界态的产生。长链极限下的连续描述揭示了相结构的本质，磁化态可用孤子描述。这为理解和设计具有可调磁性的分子材料提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study magnets composed of molecules with an arbitrary number $m$ of magnetically active atoms in a chain configuration interacting with nearest-neighbor couplings. We derive the thermodynamics of a chain of spin-$j$ $SU(2)$ magnetic atoms using a mean-field approach and determine the equilibrium states and stability conditions. The system exhibits a rich phase structure with a cascade of ferromagnetic phase transitions, their number ranging from $m$, for maximal ferromagnets, to zero, for paramagnets, depending on the relative strengths of the couplings. In the presence of impurities (modified couplings) at the ends of the chain, boundary states emerge that coexist with the paramagnetic phase or the fully ferromagnetic phase. For long chains, a continuum approximation can be taken that clarifies the structure of the phases. In the continuum description, magnetized states at temperatures lower than the critical temperature at which they appear correspond to kink solitons.

</details>


### [106] [Prescriptive Master Integrals of Maximal Weight at Two Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10672)
*Jacob L. Bourjaily*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了四维无质量粒子的平面主积分的张成集，包含所有两圈最大权重贡献，并确保每个红外发散区域由特定主积分匹配，其余主积分在四维中明显有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子场论的圈图计算中，需要处理红外发散问题。现有的主积分基底可能混合了发散和有限的贡献，不利于系统地分离和控制红外奇异性。因此需要构造一个改进的主积分基底，使得红外发散结构更加清晰，每个独立的红外发散区域能够被特定的主积分单独表示，而其他主积分保持有限性。

Method: 采用了以下方法构建主积分基底：1) 识别四维无质量粒子体系中两圈层次的所有平面费曼图；2) 系统地分析红外发散的独立区域结构；3) 选择纯函数（pure function）形式的主积分作为基底元素；4) 确保基底的张成性质，涵盖所有最大权重（maximal-weight）的贡献；5) 建立红外发散区域与特定主积分的一一对应关系，使其他主积分在四维时空中明显保持有限。

Result: 成功构建了满足要求的主积分张成集，具有以下特性：1) 所有主积分都是纯函数形式；2) 完整包含两圈的所有最大权重贡献；3) 每个独立的红外发散区域都由特定的主积分单独匹配；4) 除了与红外发散区域对应的主积分外，其余主积分在四维中都是明显有限的。这种结构使得红外奇异性的分布变得清晰和可控。

Conclusion: 本文提出的主积分基底构造方案为处理四维无质量粒子的两圈计算提供了一个优化的框架。通过将红外发散与有限贡献明确分离，这个纯函数基底不仅保留了最大权重项的完整信息，还显著简化了红外结构的分析。这种方法对于高精度的量子场论计算和散射振幅研究具有重要的理论和实用价值。

Abstract: We describe the construction of a spanning set of individually pure, planar master integrals involving massless particles in four dimensions which include all maximal-weight contributions at two loops. In this basis, every independent region associated with infrared divergence is individually matched by specific masters, with all other masters being manifestly finite in four dimensions.

</details>


### [107] [Gluons and Knots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10673)
*Alfredo Guevara*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证了单负螺旋度胶子与任意多正螺旋度胶子的散射振幅具有拓扑性质,通过将wall-crossing和Weinberg软定理重新诠释为扭结的skein关系和Reidemeister移动,构建了基于HOMFLY-PT多项式的态计数描述,并通过Lean形式化证明和多种运动学构型(包括星型构型给出Catalan数)进行了严格验证,建立了散射振幅与拓扑场论/弦论之间的精确联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射振幅理论需要更深层次的数学结构来理解其本质。传统计算方法复杂且难以揭示振幅的内在对称性和拓扑性质。研究者希望建立散射振幅与拓扑场论之间的桥梁,特别是利用Yangian不变性和Wilson圈理论,将高能物理中的散射过程与纽结理论、拓扑不变量联系起来,从而提供更简洁、更具普适性的计算框架和物理图像。

Method: 1. 理论框架构建:论证特定螺旋度构型(一个负螺旋度胶子和多个正螺旋度胶子)的散射振幅在运动学空间的区室内为常数,共振位于边界墙,因子分解由wall-crossing决定;2. 几何化描述:利用Yangian不变性将振幅表示为2维零Wilson圈,并将其提升为正定且横向的3维链环;3. 拓扑重诠释:将wall-crossing现象和Weinberg软定理重新解释为扭结的skein关系和Reidemeister移动;4. 态计数构造:通过累加子扭结HOMFLY-PT多项式的"角点"构建态计数描述;5. 形式化验证:使用Lean证明助手构建依赖图(DAG)进行严格推导,明确列出包括Reidemeister III不变性在内的精确假设;6. 数值验证:在多种运动学构型(特别是新颖的"星型"构型)下进行详尽测试,验证振幅和态计数与扭结理论文献的一致性。

Result: 1. 成功建立了散射振幅的拓扑性质描述,证明了其在运动学空间区室内的常数性;2. 构建了基于HOMFLY-PT多项式的新公式,将散射振幅与扭结不变量精确关联;3. 在星型运动学构型下,振幅和态计数均表现为Catalan数,与扭结理论文献完全吻合;4. 通过Lean形式化系统完成了严格的数学推导和验证;5. 在经典极限之外,为散射振幅与拓扑场论、弦论之间建立了精确但仍为猜想性质的联系;6. 所有测试结果均通过详尽验证,支持理论框架的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将特定螺旋度构型的胶子散射振幅问题转化为拓扑问题,通过Wilson圈、扭结理论和HOMFLY-PT多项式建立了散射振幅与拓扑不变量之间的深刻联系。形式化证明和多种运动学构型的数值验证(特别是Catalan数的出现)为这一理论框架提供了强有力的支持。研究不仅为散射振幅计算提供了新的拓扑工具,也为量子场论与拓扑场论/弦论的统一理解开辟了新路径,尽管超越经典极限的联系仍处于猜想阶段,但已建立了精确的数学框架和验证方法,为未来研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We argue that the scattering amplitude of a single minus helicity gluon with arbitrarily many positive helicity gluons is topological. The amplitude is constant in chambers of kinematic space, its resonances are located at walls and its factorization is determined by wall-crossing. Yangian invariance dictates the amplitude to be determined by a 2d null Wilson loop that can be lifted into a positive and transverse 3d link. By reinterpreting wall-crossing and the Weinberg soft theorem as skein relations and Reidemeister moves of the subknots, we construct a state-counting description consisting of adding the `corners' of their HOMFLY-PT polynomials. Beyond the classical limit, we argue that this provides a conjectured but precise connection to topological field and string theory.
  The new formula is rigorously derived via a Lean-formalized dependency graph (DAG) as detailed in a companion paper, laying out the precise assumptions including Reidemeister III invariance. It is further tested exhaustively, including novel `star' kinematics where the amplitude and state count are shown to become Catalan numbers, in agreement with knot theory literature.

</details>


### [108] [Hawking Amplitudes: Subtle Points About Saddle Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10678)
*Owen Lear,Daniel McLoughlin,Sebastian Mizera,John Staunton*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究霍金辐射振幅中半经典近似与弱场近似的相互作用，发现这两种极限不可交换，并提出了基于Stokes线确定热谱温度的新方法，成功计算了多种黑洞背景下的半经典霍金振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 霍金振幅为利用散射振幅的在壳技术确定黑洞霍金谱提供了新途径，但此前使用的半经典近似和弱场近似之间的相互作用及其适用性尚未被充分理解，需要澄清这些近似方法中的微妙问题，特别是鞍点分析中的技术细节。

Method: 通过系统研究半经典极限和弱场极限的非交换性，仔细分析控制半经典极限的鞍点方法中的微妙之处。解决这些问题后，在多种黑洞时空背景(包括Tangherlini度规、Reissner-Nordström度规和Hernquist度规)中计算半经典霍金振幅。描述不同能量下主导霍金振幅的鞍点贡献，并基于霍金振幅的Stokes线提出确定热谱温度的新方法。

Result: 成功计算了Tangherlini黑洞、Reissner-Nordström黑洞和Hernquist度规等多种黑洞背景下的半经典霍金振幅。确认了半经典近似与弱场近似不可交换这一重要发现。识别出不同能量区域中主导霍金振幅的鞍点贡献结构。发展出基于Stokes线分析确定霍金温度的新技术方法。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了霍金振幅计算中半经典极限与弱场极限的非交换性,这对正确理解鞍点分析至关重要。通过解决这些技术问题,成功将霍金振幅方法推广到多种黑洞时空,并提出了基于Stokes线的温度确定新方法,为利用现代振幅技术研究黑洞热力学提供了更完善的理论框架。

Abstract: Hawking amplitudes provide a new way of determining the Hawking spectrum of black holes, using on-shell techniques borrowed from the literature on scattering amplitudes. In this paper, we investigate the interplay between the semi-classical and weak-field approximations previously used to compute Hawking amplitudes. We find that these limits do not necessarily commute, highlighting subtleties with the saddle point analyses governing the semi-classical limit. Resolving these issues allows us to compute semi-classical Hawking amplitudes in a range of black-hole backgrounds, including Tangherlini, Reissner-Nordström, and Hernquist metrics. Moreover, we describe the saddle-point contributions dominating Hawking amplitudes for different energies and propose a new method for determining the temperature of the resulting thermal spectrum based on Stokes lines of Hawking amplitudes.

</details>


### [109] [Covariant BCFW for All Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10686)
*Jacob L. Bourjaily,Michael Plesser*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在大质量旋量-螺旋度形式体系中提出了一种新的大质量旋量构造方法,可连接不同基底选择,并推导出适用于任意质量粒子的广义BCFW位移公式,为四维大质量粒子散射振幅的在壳递归计算提供了更统一的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在涉及大质量粒子的散射振幅计算中,存在多种基底表示选择,且传统BCFW递归方法主要针对无质量粒子发展。需要建立一个统一的形式体系来处理大质量和无质量粒子的混合情况,使在壳递归方法能够更连贯地应用于包含大质量粒子的四维振幅计算。

Method: 采用大质量旋量-螺旋度形式体系(massive spinor-helicity formalism),构造了一种新颖的大质量旋量表示方法。该方法能够在涉及大质量粒子振幅表示的多种基底选择之间建立联系,并在此基础上推导出广义的BCFW位移(BCFW shift)公式,该公式不依赖于参与粒子是大质量还是无质量。

Result: 成功推导出一个新的广义BCFW位移公式,该公式对大质量和无质量粒子具有普适性,无论涉及的粒子质量如何都能统一处理。建立了不同大质量旋量基底表示之间的连接关系,为实际计算提供了灵活的工具。

Conclusion: 该形式体系为四维空间中包含大质量粒子的散射振幅计算提供了更加连贯和统一的在壳递归实现方案。新的大质量旋量构造方法和广义BCFW位移公式能够促进涉及大质量粒子的振幅计算方法的发展,使理论计算更加系统化和高效化。

Abstract: We use the massive spinor-helicity formalism to describe a novel construction for massive spinors which allows one to connect the many choices of bases useful in the representation of amplitudes involving massive particles, and we use this to derive a new formulation for the general BCFW shift that is valid regardless whether or which of the particles involved are massive or massless. This formalism should help facilitate a more coherent implementation of on-shell recursion for computing amplitudes involving massive particles in four dimensions.

</details>


### [110] [General Spin-1 Dynamics During Inflation: Microcausality Constraints and Cosmological Collider Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10693)
*Trevor Cheung,Carlos Duaso-Pueyo,David Stefanyszyn,Dong-Gang Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了在暴胀背景下传播的自旋-1场的一般理论(Boostless Proca理论),包含5个自由参数,研究了其对宇宙学关联函数的影响。发现双谱没有新信号,但三谱和高阶函数产生新的形状特征,所有5个参数原则上可测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大质量Proca理论在暴胀背景下的描述不够完整。需要构建一个与标度不变性、旋转对称性、Bunch-Davies条件和微因果性相容的自旋-1场的一般理论框架,以系统研究其在宇宙学观测中的信号特征,特别是对非高斯性的贡献。

Method: 1) 推广传统大质量Proca理论,构建包含5个自由参数(4个宇称偶项和1个宇称奇项)的Boostless Proca拉格朗日量;2) 从一开始就施加三个自由度约束;3) 通过微因果性条件(场算符在光锥外消失)要求动能项具有规范不变性;4) 将自旋-1态与暴胀子耦合;5) 系统分析其对宇宙学关联函数(双谱、三谱及高阶函数)的贡献特征。

Result: 对于双谱(bispectrum),理论没有产生新的信号,这意味着已知信号是最一般的形式;对于三谱(trispectrum)和更高阶关联函数,Boostless Proca理论产生了新的形状特征,理论中所有5个自由参数原则上都可以通过观测测量;微因果性条件约束了理论结构,要求动能项必须是规范不变的。

Conclusion: Boostless Proca理论提供了暴胀期自旋-1场的完整理论框架,为通过宇宙学观测探测新物理提供了干净的、模型无关的探针。双谱的结果确认了现有信号的完备性,而三谱及高阶函数为探测理论的5个参数提供了新的观测窗口,具有重要的宇宙学观测意义。

Abstract: We construct the general theory of a spin-1 field propagating on an inflationary background consistent with scale invariance, rotations, Bunch-Davies conditions and microcausality. Our Boostless Proca theory generalises the usual massive Proca theory to a Lagrangian with 5 free parameters: 4 corresponding to parity-even terms and a single parity-odd term. We impose three degrees of freedom from the outset and find that microcausality, the statement that field operators vanish outside the lightcone, requires the kinetic terms to be gauge invariant. We couple this spin-1 state to the inflaton and study the signatures it imprints on cosmological correlators. We find no new signals for the bispectrum meaning that the known signals are the most general thereby providing a very clean model-independent probe of new physics in this leading form of non-Gaussianity. For the trispectrum and higher-point functions, the boostless Proca theory produces new shapes with all 5 parameters in principle measurable.

</details>


### [111] [Scattering entanglement entropy between one particle species and the others](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10704)
*Yuan-Hao Zheng,Jiayin Gu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了散射过程中特定粒子种类与其余终态之间的纠缠熵，通过约化密度矩阵的分析，将冯·诺依曼熵表示为散射概率和微分截面的函数，并研究了Tsallis熵和Rényi熵的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子散射理论中，纠缠熵是描述子系统量子关联的重要物理量，但现有研究缺乏将散射过程的纠缠熵与可观测的散射概率、微分截面等物理量直接联系起来的一般性框架。需要建立一个模型无关的、适用于一般散射过程的纠缠熵计算方法。

Method: 从纯初态动量本征态出发，利用时间演化算符构造完整终态，分析特定粒子种类A的约化密度矩阵在Fock空间中的结构。证明该约化密度矩阵在零粒子、单粒子和多粒子态子空间中呈块对角化形式,且在单粒子态的动量表象下是对角的。在终态最多产生一个A粒子的假设下，推导出冯·诺依曼熵与散射概率、微分截面的关系式，并引入相空间分辨因子处理无穷维动量希尔伯特空间问题。进一步计算n≥2的Tsallis熵和Rényi熵。

Result: 成功将粒子A的冯·诺依曼熵S_A表示为散射概率和微分截面的函数，该熵同时包含总散射概率(或截面)信息和微分分布信息，后者依赖于相空间分辨因子。对于n≥2的Tsallis熵和Rényi熵，在领头阶近似下它们正比于总散射概率，但不携带微分分布信息。该结果具有模型无关性和普适性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了散射过程纠缠熵的一般性理论框架，实现了纠缠熵与可观测散射量(概率、微分截面)的直接联系。冯·诺依曼熵能够完整反映散射过程的总体和微分信息，而高阶Rényi熵和Tsallis熵在领头阶仅包含总散射概率信息。该框架适用于终态产生次领头多重度粒子的一般散射过程,为从量子信息角度理解散射物理提供了新工具。

Abstract: We construct the entanglement entropy of a general scattering process between one particle species (denoted as $A$) and the rest of the final state. Assuming a pure initial momentum eigenstate, we show that the reduced density matrix in particle $A$'s Fock space is block-diagonal in zero-, one- and multi-particle states, and is diagonal in the momentum basis for the one-particle state. As a result, we could write down the Von Neumann entropy of particle $A$, $S_A$, in terms of scattering probabilities and differential cross sections, assuming no more than one particle $A$ is produced in the final state. $S_A$ contains the information of both the total scattering probabilities (or cross sections) and the differential distributions --- the latter depends on a phase-space resolution factor as a result of the infinite-dimensional momentum Hilbert space. Our result is general and model-independent since we consider the full final state from time evolution ($S|i\rangle$) rather than a specific outcome, and is applicable as long as the probability for producing multiple particle $A$ is subleading. We also obtain the $n\geq2$ Tsallis and Rényi entropies in our framework and find out that, at the leading order, they are proportional to the total scattering probability and carry no differential information.

</details>


### [112] [Large Charge Jets in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM Energy Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10707)
*Alba Grassi,Cristoforo Iossa,Luigi Tizzano,Alexander Zhiboedov*

Main category: hep-th

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 有限耦合能量-能量关联函数展现出随角度参数z变化的层次化物理区间结构:(1)小角度真空主导区:z≪λ^(-1);(2)非单调介质诱导瞬态区:λz~1;(3)大荷喷注区:λ^(-1)≲z≲λ^(-1/3),呈现幂律行为~1/(λz³);(4)大角度平台区:z≳λ^(-1/3),关联函数趋于角度无关的常数平台。

Conclusion: 本研究成功揭示了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中重规范算符态的能量-能量关联函数在有限耦合下的完整物理图景。通过OPE分析方法,确立了从小角度到大角度四个不同物理区间的精确标度行为,其中大荷喷注区的1/(λz³)幂律行为和大角度平台结构是该系统的特征性质,反映了共形对称性破缺和希格斯机制的深刻影响。

Abstract: We compute the energy-energy correlator in a state created by a heavy canonical operator of scaling dimension $Δ_H$ in $\mathcal{N}=4$ $SU(N)$ SYM, at fixed $N$ and arbitrary values of the large charge 't Hooft coupling $λ\sim Δ_H g_{\rm YM}^2$. The state created by the canonical operator triggers spontaneous breaking of conformal symmetry and the Higgs mechanism, with squared W-boson masses proportional to $λ$. We start with the known finite-coupling result for the underlying four-point function of local operators, perform the detector and Fourier transforms and express the resulting energy-energy correlator as an infinite OPE sum in the source-detector channel, which we then analyze both numerically and analytically. We show that the finite-coupling energy-energy correlator exhibits a hierarchy of distinct physical regimes as a function of $z=(1-\cosθ)/2$, where $θ$ is the angle between the detectors. A small-angle vacuum-dominated behavior at $z\llλ^{-1}$ is followed by a non-monotonic medium-induced transient behavior at $λz \sim1$. A large charge jet regime follows, where the energy-energy correlator displays a power-law behavior $\sim1/(λz^3)$ for $λ^{-1}\lesssim z \lesssim λ^{-1/3}$, before approaching an angle-independent plateau at larger angles.

Result: 成功重构了任意6维(1,0)超引力理论的原始BPS弦生成元和完整闭合BPS弦锥。该重构为低能理论提供了新的约束条件，能够排除通过所有先前已知的局部晶格和反常一致性条件的候选有效场论。对于具有几何张量基的理论，确定了对应Kähler曲面的完整Mori锥。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了基于有效场论数据系统重构BPS弦锥的完整框架，不仅确定了六维(1,0)超引力理论中的完整BPS弦谱结构，还为理论的一致性提供了比传统局部晶格和反常条件更强的新约束。该方法在几何情形下与Kähler曲面的Mori锥建立了直接联系，为理解六维超引力理论的几何和物理性质提供了重要工具。

Abstract: The tensor branch of six-dimensional $\mathcal N=(1,0)$ supergravity is characterized by a spectrum of BPS strings charged under the two-form gauge fields. Their charges span a cone in the tensor charge lattice, whose extremal rays are generated by primitive BPS string charges that become tensionless at the boundaries of the tensor moduli space. We develop a systematic procedure, based entirely on effective field theory data, to reconstruct the primitive BPS string generators and thereby determine the complete closed BPS string cone of any 6d $(1,0)$ supergravity theory. The key ingredients are the universal {\it H}-string, which allows us to isolate a fixed sector determined by classified LST blocks and gauge anomaly data from the remaining gauge-neutral E-string sector, and a BPS refinement of the Completeness Conjecture that ensures population of the relevant charge rays. We further show that this reconstruction provides new constraints on the low-energy theory and can rule out candidate EFTs that pass all previously known local lattice and anomaly consistency conditions. Separately, for theories admitting geometric tensor bases, we explain how the same reconstruction determines the complete Mori cone of the corresponding Kähler surface.

### [114] [All-multiplicity completion of maximally supersymmetric gauge amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10712)

Result: 对于正的领头四点接触系数,对数参数化中的偶数阶系数被完全固定,而奇数阶系数的每个实数序列都可以在所有导数阶上实现完备化。证明了六点条件对于这种完备化是必要且充分的。一旦高点接触相互作用与相同的对称性和因子化条件一致地选定,更高多重性的一致性对这些四点系数不施加进一步的限制。

Conclusion: 六点约束条件对于平面四维最大超对称规范理论中任意多重性的四点振幅完备化是必要且充分的。偶数阶系数被完全确定,奇数阶系数保持自由但受局域性和因子化约束。高于六点的多重性一致性不对四点系数施加额外限制,这为理解超对称规范理论的振幅结构提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We show that the known six-point constraints on four-point amplitudes are sufficient for completion at arbitrary multiplicity in planar, four-dimensional maximally supersymmetric gauge theory. We construct massless tree amplitudes as formal series in the derivative expansion, with the standard supersymmetric Yang-Mills three-point amplitudes, unbroken R-symmetry, and parity invariance of amplitudes with only external scalars, holding the scalar labels fixed. For a positive leading four-point contact coefficient, the even coefficients in the logarithmic parameterization are fixed, while every real sequence of odd coefficients is realized by a completion at all derivative orders. The construction uses the field-theory Kawai-Lewellen-Tye bilinear and normalized matrices of sphere integrals. Exponentiating homogeneous components of their matrix logarithms varies the odd coefficients independently while preserving locality and factorization through the complete massless multiplet. The six-point conditions are therefore necessary and sufficient for such a completion. Higher-multiplicity consistency imposes no further restrictions on these four-point coefficients once the higher-point contact interactions are chosen consistently with the same symmetries and factorization conditions.

</details>


### [115] [Generalised Gibbs Ensembles from the ODE/IM Correspondence: the Lee--Yang Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10716)
*Sujay K. Ashok,Max Downing,Faisal Karimi,Adarsh Sudhakar,Roberto Tateo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统性方案，用于构造广义Gibbs系综的ODE/IM对应关系，并应用于Lee-Yang共形场论的手征扇区。通过变形三次振子编码化学势，并利用WKB方法预测变形本征值，在自旋-5系综中验证了该方案的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Gibbs系综在可积模型中具有重要意义，但其ODE/IM对应关系缺乏系统性的构造方案。现有研究需要一种通用方法来建立化学势与微分方程变形之间的明确联系，并能够预测局域守恒荷的变形本征值。

Result: 在两种情况下，Stokes乘子均满足Lee-Yang Y-系统；变形本征值在检验的阶数内与独立的共形场论计算结果匹配；基态能量的Borel重求和在化学势的多个数量级范围内与数值TBA解高度吻合，验证精度达到多个数量级。

Conclusion: 成功建立了广义Gibbs系综的ODE/IM对应关系的系统性构造方案，并在Lee-Yang共形场论中得到验证。该方案仅依赖ODE/IM对应关系的一般特征，具有普适性，可应用于其他可积模型的广义Gibbs系综研究，为探索可积系统的非平衡统计力学提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We introduce a systematic scheme for constructing the ODE/IM counterpart of generalised Gibbs ensembles, and apply it to the chiral sector of the Lee--Yang conformal field theory. We encode the chemical potentials in a deformation of the cubic oscillator, fixed order by order by requiring that the large-energy WKB period reproduces exactly the driving term of the corresponding thermodynamic Bethe ansatz equation; the subleading WKB periods then yield, as predictions, the deformed eigenvalues of the local conserved charges. We work out the spin-5 ensemble in detail for two states of the model: the ground state, in the module of the primary $φ$, dual to the deformed cubic oscillator, and the primary of the identity module, dual to the same oscillator supplemented by an apparent singularity, a double pole whose position is fixed by a trivial-monodromy condition. In both cases, the Stokes multiplier obeys the Lee--Yang Y-system, and the deformed eigenvalues match, to the orders checked, an independent conformal-field-theory computation. The Borel resummation of the resulting series for the ground-state energy reproduces the numerical TBA solution over many orders of magnitude in the chemical potential. The scheme relies only on general features of the ODE/IM correspondence and applies to generalised Gibbs ensembles in other integrable models.

</details>


### [116] [Carroll-Weyl gauged null strings in AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10717)
*Sarthak Duary,Sourav Maji*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS_D空间Poincaré坐标系中的Carroll-Weyl规范零弦及其BRST量子化，通过径向适配基构造了扩展的BMS_3代数结构，分类了四个独立的中心扩张上同调类，并在正则最高权一阶表示中通过BRST协循环抵消条件确定了临界维度D=27。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探索AdS空间中Carroll-Weyl零弦理论的量子化问题。传统的平直时空Carroll-Weyl理论存在膨胀约束带来的额外反常通道，而在AdS背景下，利用径向共形Killing矢量的同质性质可能导致不同的代数结构和反常结构。研究这种几何背景对零弦量子化的影响，特别是剩余对称代数及其中心扩张的分类，对理解全息对偶和引力的渐近对称性具有重要意义。

Method: 1. 在Poincaré坐标下构造径向共形Killing矢量K=∂_z，通过适当的共形关联标架使其成为同质矢量；2. 对同质哈密顿约束进行场依赖的重新标度，导出径向适配基{M, L, S}，其中M在Carroll-Weyl径向流下保持不变；3. 分析这些模式形成的代数结构，识别出由BMS_3代数和阿贝尔权重为1的流构成的扩展代数；4. 利用上同调方法系统分类该剩余代数允许的中心扩张，在LL、LM、LS、SS四个通道中找到独立的上同调类；5. 构造包含径向Carroll-Weyl约束鬼对的完整BRST复形；6. 在正则最高权一阶表示中计算中心扩张的实现，并通过BRST协循环抵消条件确定临界维度。

Result: 研究发现径向适配基的模式形成扩展的BMS_3代数，存在四个独立的中心扩张上同调类(c_LL, c_LM, c_LS, c_SS)。在正则最高权一阶表示中，只有Virasoro型中心扩张被实现，具体数值为(2D-54, 0, 0, 0)。通过要求非平凡BRST协循环抵消，得到临界维度D=27。这一结果在结构上不同于最小平直Carroll-Weyl理论，后者由于膨胀约束X·P的存在会产生额外的反常通道。

Conclusion: 本文通过BRST量子化方法研究了AdS_D空间Poincaré坐标系中的Carroll-Weyl规范零弦，确定了其剩余对称代数为扩展的BMS_3结构，并系统分类了中心扩张。在正则最高权一阶表示中，仅Virasoro型中心扩张非零，BRST一致性条件给出临界维度D=27。这一结果揭示了AdS背景下Carroll-Weyl零弦理论具有与平直时空理论结构性不同的反常结构，为理解全息框架下的零弦理论和渐近对称性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study the Carroll-Weyl gauged null string in the Poincaré patch of $\mathrm{AdS}_D$ and its BRST quantization. In Poincaré coordinates $X^A=(z,x^i)$, the radial conformal Killing vector $K=\partial_z$ becomes homothetic in a suitable conformally related frame. A field-dependent rescaling of the homothetic Hamiltonian constraint then leads to the radial-adapted basis $\mathcal{M}=\frac{1}{2}(P_z^2+P_iP^i)$, $\mathcal{L}=-P_AX'^A$, and $\mathcal{S}=P_z$, with $\mathcal{M}$ invariant along the Carroll-Weyl radial flow. Their modes form a $\mathrm{BMS}_3$ algebra extended by an abelian weight-one current. We classify the central extensions allowed by this residual algebra and find four independent cohomology classes in the $LL$, $LM$, $LS$, and $SS$ channels. We then construct the associated BRST complex, including the ghost pair for the radial Carroll-Weyl constraint. In the canonical highest-weight first-order representation, only the Virasoro-type central extension is realized: $(c_{LL},c_{LM},c_{LS},c_{SS})=(2D-54,0,0,0)$. Cancellation of the corresponding nontrivial BRST cocycle therefore gives $D=27$ in this representation. This differs structurally from the minimal flat Carroll-Weyl theory, where the dilation constraint $X\cdot P$ gives extra anomaly channels.

</details>


### [117] [Surface defects in $\mathcal{N}=4$ Super Yang-Mills: exact results and holographic bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10723)
*Lorenzo Bianchi,Davide Bonomi,Maddalena Ferragatta,Valentina Forini*

TL;DR: 研究了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中存在半BPS表面缺陷时手征初级算符的两点函数,通过求解超共形Ward恒等式、构造超共形块、利用手征代数映射和缺陷色散关系,重构了大N强耦合极限下的完整关联函数,并提取了大量新的缺陷CFT数据。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中引入半BPS表面缺陷后,该缺陷仅保留横向旋转与整体U(1) R对称性的对角组合,使得关联函数依赖于额外的交叉比并分解为不同角荷扇区。这类破缺横向旋转对称性的缺陷产生了带电荷扇区,这在保持横向旋转的缺陷中没有对应物。研究这类系统的两点函数需要发展新的理论工具,包括构造所有扇区(特别是带电扇区)的超共形块,并建立非微扰的求解方法。

Method: 1) 求解超共形Ward恒等式,构造所有扇区(包括带电扇区)的体超共形块和缺陷超共形块;2) 将全纯扇区映射到手征代数关联函数,并将此映射推广到破缺横向旋转的缺陷,这需要重新定义二维流代数的模式,以此非微扰地固定全纯扇区和带电扇区;3) 利用缺陷色散关系在大N强耦合领极限下重构维度为二的手征初级算符的完整关联函数;4) 在弱耦合极限展开结果并与已知结果对比验证;5) 通过旋转不变轨迹上的独立Witten图自举方法重现强耦合结果。

Result: 成功构造了所有扇区的超共形块,在大N强耦合极限下重构了维度为二的手征初级算符的完整关联函数。在带电扇区中长多重态缺失,给出的结果在N和耦合常数上都是猜想精确的。弱耦合展开与已知结果完美吻合。从这些关联函数中提取了无穷多个新的缺陷CFT数据。通过Witten图自举独立重现了强耦合结果。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中存在破缺横向旋转对称性的半BPS表面缺陷时手征初级算符两点函数的计算问题。通过发展手征代数映射、超共形块构造和缺陷色散关系等多种技术手段,获得了大N强耦合极限下的精确结果,并在带电扇区给出了猜想精确解。这些结果不仅与已知的弱耦合计算一致,还通过Witten图自举得到独立验证,为理解缺陷共形场论提供了大量新的数据和深刻见解。

Abstract: We study two-point functions of chiral primary operators in $\mathcal{N}=4$ Super Yang-Mills in the presence of a half-BPS surface defect. Such a defect preserves only the diagonal combination of transverse rotations and of a global U(1) R-symmetry, so that the correlator depends on one additional cross-ratio and splits into sectors of definite angular charge. We solve the superconformal Ward identities and construct the bulk and defect superconformal blocks in all sectors, including the charged ones, which have no analogue for defects preserving transverse rotations. The solution contains a holomorphic sector, which we fix non-perturbatively by mapping it to a chiral algebra correlator. We extend this map to defects breaking transverse rotations, where it requires a redefinition of the modes of the two-dimensional current algebra, and use it to fix the charged sectors as well. We then use the defect dispersion relation to reconstruct the full correlator of the dimension-two chiral primary at leading order at large N and strong coupling. In the charged sectors long multiplets are absent and the answer is conjecturally exact in both N and the coupling; expanding it at weak coupling, we find perfect agreement with known results. From these correlators we extract infinitely many new pieces of defect CFT data. Finally, we reproduce the strong-coupling answer by an independent Witten diagram bootstrap on the rotation-invariant locus.

</details>


### [118] [The hidden sunrise in the energy-energy correlator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10734)
*Matthew D. Schwartz,Xiaoyuan Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文完成了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中能量-能量关联函数(EEC)在次次领头阶(NNLO)的完整解析计算,特别是处理了包含椭圆曲线的椭圆扇区,并将结果用迭代Eisenstein积分表示,为研究EEC的Landau自举和QCD中的椭圆扇区提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量-能量关联函数(EEC)是少数可以高阶解析计算的对撞机可观测量之一,它作为能量加权截面揭示了散射振幅无法展现的量子场论特征。先前在N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中EEC的NNLO计算存在一个包含椭圆曲线的双重积分未能完全解析,这限制了对其椭圆扇区的深入理解和高效数值评估,也阻碍了对QCD中EEC椭圆扇区的研究。

Method: 研究采用以下方法:(1)分析EEC中椭圆扇区与sunrise费曼积分的关系,证明二者的j-不变量在Möbius映射下完全一致;(2)确认EEC的椭圆扇区存在于sunrise积分的Γ_1(6)模曲线上;(3)将椭圆扇区结果用迭代Eisenstein积分表示,这种表示形式可以快速高精度数值评估;(4)利用解析形式首次研究EEC的Landau自举方法;(5)部分技术计算在人类监督下使用AI完成。

Result: 成功获得了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中EEC在NNLO阶的完整解析结果,包括此前未解决的椭圆扇区部分。证明了EEC椭圆扇区与sunrise积分具有相同的j-不变量和模结构(Γ_1(6)模曲线)。建立了基于迭代Eisenstein积分的表示方法,实现了快速高精度数值计算。首次完成了EEC Landau自举的研究,并为理解QCD中EEC的椭圆扇区函数空间提供了具体的理论工具。

Conclusion: 本文通过完整解析N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中EEC的NNLO椭圆扇区,揭示了其与sunrise积分的深刻数学联系,证明两者共享相同的Γ_1(6)模曲线结构。采用迭代Eisenstein积分的表示方法不仅解决了高精度数值评估问题,还为EEC Landau自举研究开辟了新途径。更重要的是,N=4 SYM理论中对椭圆扇区函数空间的理解为处理物理上更相关的QCD理论中的EEC椭圆扇区提供了重要的理论参照和技术手段,推进了对撞机可观测量高阶计算的理论发展。

Abstract: The energy-energy correlator (EEC) is one of a handful of collider observables that can be computed analytically to high orders. As an energy-weighted cross-section, it exposes features of quantum field theory that scattering amplitudes do not, and it can be compared directly to data. As with scattering amplitudes, the analytic expressions require special functions beyond polylogarithms. In Henn et al., the EEC was computed to next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) for $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills (SYM) theory, and the result is expressed in terms of both harmonic polylogarithms (HPLs) and one two-fold integral, which contains elliptic curves. In this work, we report a complete result including the remaining elliptic sector, which is closely related to the sunrise Feynman integrals. We find the $j$-invariants of the EEC and the sunrise agree identically under a Möbius map, and thus the elliptic sector in the EEC lives on the $Γ_1(6)$ modular curve of the sunrise integral. We then express the answer in terms of iterated Eisenstein integrals, which can be evaluated to high precision quickly. The analytic form also allows a first study of the EEC Landau bootstrap, and the understanding of its function space in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM offers a concrete handle on the elliptic sector of the EEC in QCD. Many of the technical results in this paper were completed with AI under human supervision.

</details>


### [119] [Polynomial and Eigenspace Factorisations for the One-loop Dilatation Operator at Finite $N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10745)
*Adrian Padellaro,Sanjaye Ramgoolam,Ryo Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了𝒩=4超杨-米尔斯理论su(2)扇区中有限N规范群U(N)或SU(N)下的规范不变双矩阵多项式,建立了单圈膨胀算符的特征多项式整除性质,揭示了U(N)与SU(N)理论间本征态的塔状结构关系,并提出了SU(N)理论在有限N下的置换框架猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在𝒩=4超杨-米尔斯理论中,研究有限N规范群下su(2)扇区的规范不变态空间结构及其单圈膨胀算符的谱性质是一个基础问题。特别需要理解:1)当N<L时态空间如何通过杨图截断从无限N情况约化;2)U(N)与SU(N)理论的本征态空间之间存在何种深层联系;3)如何为SU(N)理论建立直接的置换代数框架以处理有限N的谱问题。

Method: 采用置换中心化子代数的方法构造态空间𝒜_N(L)作为对称群S_L群代数的子代数直和。对于N≥L情况,态空间与N无关;对N<L情况,通过杨图截断定义子空间。利用特征多项式分析单圈膨胀算符的谱结构,通过单字母迹的乘法运算构造从SU(N)到U(N)的本征态塔。提出SU(N)不变多项式空间同构于𝒜_N(L)与错排张成子空间交集的猜想,并通过维数计数验证。最后在该交集上定义单圈膨胀算符并计算谱以验证一致性。

Result: 证明了两个主要结果:1)𝒜_N(L)上单圈膨胀算符的特征多项式整除从𝒜(L)延拓到N<L的特征多项式;2)SU(N)理论中每个单圈膨胀算符的本征态通过单字母迹的乘法在U(N)理论中生成无穷本征态塔,这些塔定义了由迹数目标记的本征空间因子化。交集猜想得到维数计数的支持,基于该猜想计算的谱与U(N)理论本征空间因子化的预测一致。

Conclusion: 本文为有限N的𝒩=4超杨-米尔斯理论su(2)扇区建立了完整的置换代数框架,揭示了U(N)与SU(N)理论之间通过本征态塔结构的深刻联系,证明了特征多项式的整除性质。提出的交集猜想为SU(N)理论提供了直接的置换表述,所定义的单圈膨胀算符给出与U(N)理论一致的谱结构,为进一步研究有限N效应和规范/弦对偶提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: We study gauge-invariant two-matrix polynomials of degree $L$, corresponding to the states in the $su(2)$ sector of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory with gauge group $U(N)$ or $SU(N)$ at finite $N$. For the $U(N)$ theory, the state space has the structure of an algebra $\mathcal{A}_N(L)$, which is a direct sum of permutation centraliser algebras, i.e. sub-algebras of the group algebra $\mathbb{C}[S_L]$ of $S_L$. For $N \ge L$, $\mathcal{A}_N (L) = \mathcal{A} (L)$ is independent of $N$, and the one-loop dilatation operator acts on $\mathcal{A}(L)$ with $N$ as a parameter. For $N < L$, $\mathcal{A}_N (L)$ is a subspace of $\mathcal{A}(L)$ defined using a finite-$N$ cutoff on Young diagrams with $L$ boxes. We establish two results on the eigenspaces of the one-loop dilatation operator. Our first main result is that the characteristic polynomial of the one-loop dilatation operator on $\mathcal{A}_N(L)$ divides the characteristic polynomial from $\mathcal{A}(L)$, continued to $N < L$. Our second result is that each eigenstate of the one-loop dilatation operator in the $SU(N)$ theory generates an infinite tower of eigenstates in the $U(N)$ theory, through the multiplication of single-letter traces. These towers define an eigenspace factorisation of the $U(N)$ theory labelled by the number of such traces. Further, we conjecture that the space of $SU(N)$-invariant polynomials in two traceless matrices at finite $N$ is isomorphic to the intersection of $\mathcal{A}_N(L)$ with the subspace of $\mathbb{C}[S_L]$ spanned by derangements. This intersection conjecture is supported by dimension counting. Finally, we propose a one-loop dilatation operator on the intersection, thus providing a direct permutation framework for the $SU(N)$ spectral problem at finite $N$. The computed spectra based on the proposal agree with the prediction from the eigenspace factorisation in the $U(N)$ theory.

</details>


### [120] [Fourier analysis of compact oscillons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10746)
*Pawel Klimas,Leandro Roza Livramento,João Saldanha Streibel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用傅里叶方法推导了signum-Gordon模型中精确紧致oscillon的新解，将其分解为基频及其奇次谐波的无穷级数，并证明该级数一致收敛。研究还推广到具有周期性移动边界的摆动紧致oscillon，揭示了偶数阶模式在产生摆动运动中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准解析势中对应的无穷微扰级数无法收敛，且现有理论存在关于空间局域化小振幅周期态的不存在定理。需要一种新的方法来精确描述signum-Gordon模型中的紧致oscillon结构，并理解其动力学特性，特别是摆动oscillon的运动机制。

Method: 采用傅里叶方法将oscillon分解为由基频和奇次高阶谐波组成的无穷离散模式的一致收敛级数。通过截断傅里叶级数评估近似精度，分析主模式以及前两个非零谐波模式的贡献。将该框架推广到具有周期性移动边界的摆动紧致oscillon，引入偶数阶模式与奇数源项的耦合机制来描述动态调制。

Result: 成功推导出signum-Gordon模型中精确紧致oscillon的新解，该解规避了空间局域化小振幅周期态的不存在定理。单个主模式就能提供remarkably精确的近似，包含前两个非零谐波模式时精度显著提升。对于摆动oscillon，偶数阶模式与奇数源项的耦合成功产生了摆动运动，尽管空间傅里叶轮廓本身与时间无关。

Conclusion: 傅里叶方法为signum-Gordon模型中紧致oscillon提供了精确且收敛的描述，克服了传统微扰方法的局限性。主模式的高精度近似表明oscillon结构主要由基频主导，少量谐波即可显著改善精度。摆动oscillon的成功描述揭示了偶数阶模式在产生周期性空间调制中的物理机制，为理解非线性场论中的局域化周期解提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We present a new derivation of the exact compact oscillon in the signum-Gordon model using Fourier methods. This approach decomposes the oscillon into an infinite, uniformly convergent series of discrete modes consisting of a fundamental frequency and its odd higher harmonics, in contrast to standard analytic potentials, where the corresponding infinite perturbative series fails to converge. Crucially, our oscillon evades the non-existence theorem for spatially localized, small-amplitude periodic states. We investigate the accuracy of truncating the Fourier series and find that the primary mode alone provides a remarkably accurate approximation of the exact solution, with agreement improving significantly when the first two non-vanishing harmonic modes are included. Furthermore, we generalize this framework to a swaying compact oscillon with a periodically moving boundary. We show that describing such an oscillon requires even-order modes, whose coupling with the odd source terms induces a dynamic modulation that generates the swaying motion, even though the spatial Fourier profiles themselves are time-independent.

</details>


### [121] [Reconstruction of Black Hole Islands from Hawking Radiation via the Algebraic Petz Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10769)
*Niloofar Vardian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过代数Petz映射构建了岛屿重构协议,解决了黑洞视界内部算符如何从霍金辐射中重构的问题,为岛屿公式提供了严格的操作字典,证明了黑洞内部信息可被解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 岛屿方案虽然保证了蒸发黑洞的幺正Page曲线,但视界深处算符与渐近霍金辐射之间的映射关系尚未建立。由于内部区域因果断开且高度动力学演化,依赖全局等距或连续模流的标准纠缠楔重构方法失效,需要新的重构协议来实现信息解码。

Method: 利用模理论(modular theory)和代数Petz映射构建岛屿重构协议。具体方法包括:(1)将岛屿算符反射到可访问的补空间;(2)将算符输运到边界;(3)将算符映射到辐射代数中。整个过程采用纯代数推导,不依赖全局对称性或连续模流。

Result: 成功构建了从黑洞内部到霍金辐射的算符映射协议,该纯代数推导自然地产生了Papadodimas-Raju镜像算符的结构。该框架为岛屿公式提供了严格的操作性字典,建立了视界内部算符与边界辐射算符之间的明确对应关系。

Conclusion: 通过代数Petz映射的岛屿重构协议解决了黑洞信息佯谬中的关键技术问题,严格证明了被困在黑洞内部的信息可以从霍金辐射中解码和重构。该方法克服了因果断开和动力学演化带来的障碍,为量子引力中的全息原理和黑洞信息问题提供了可操作的数学框架。

Abstract: The island proposal guarantees a unitary Page curve for evaporating black holes, implying that operators deep behind the horizon must be fully reconstructable from asymptotic Hawking radiation. However, because the interior is causally disconnected and highly dynamical, standard entanglement wedge reconstruction methods, relying on global isometries or continuous modular flow, fail to provide this mapping. We resolve this by constructing a protocol for island reconstruction using the algebraic Petz map. By using modular theory, we reflect island operators into the accessible complement, transport them to the boundary, and map them into the radiation algebra. Furthermore, we demonstrate that this purely algebraic derivation naturally yields the structure of Papadodimas-Raju mirror operators. This framework provides a rigorous operational dictionary for the island formula, proving that information trapped inside a black hole can be decoded.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond gauge-parameter dependence: Further evidence for asymptotic safety in Scalar-Tensor theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10791)
*Samuel M. P. Antunes,Antonio D. Pereira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量-张量理论在渐近安全框架下的量子化问题,通过采用依赖于动力学标量场的规范条件,验证了非平凡重整化群不动点的稳健性,并在四维时空中识别出属于高斯物质类的不动点,发现了具有2个或4个相关方向的不动点候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管过去几年在各种标量-张量模型中积累了大量关于非平凡重整化群不动点存在性的证据,但需要验证这些不动点在计算设置发生重大修改时的稳健性。特别需要超越文献中常用的标准线性协变规范,探索更具一般性的规范条件对不动点结构的影响。

Method: 采用显式依赖于动力学标量场的规范条件,而非仅仅对标准线性协变规范进行规范参数形变。通过探索不同的场参数化方案,在四维时空中寻找和分析重整化群不动点。系统研究标量场反常维度和场参数化对临界指数的影响,特别关注标量场质量和非最小标量-曲率耦合相关的临界指数。

Result: 在四维时空中识别出属于高斯物质类的不动点,这些结果与先前研究一致。发现与标量场质量和非最小标量-曲率耦合相关的临界指数的实部具有非常小的绝对值。通过对标量场反常维度和场参数化影响的全面探索,发现了具有2个或4个相关方向的不动点候选。

Conclusion: 标量-张量理论中非平凡重整化群不动点在采用依赖于动力学标量场的规范条件下表现出稳健性,验证了渐近安全框架作为非微扰可重整化量子场论的有效性。不同场参数化方案下识别出的不动点候选具有2个或4个相关方向,且关键临界指数呈现出小绝对值特征,这为标量-张量理论的量子化提供了重要的理论支持。

Abstract: Asymptotic Safety provides a promising framework for the quantization of Scalar-Tensor theories as non-perturbatively renormalizable quantum field theories. Over the past years, growing evidence has accumulated for the existence of non-trivial renormalization group fixed points in a variety of Scalar-Tensor models. In this work, we investigate the robustness of these fixed points under significant modifications of the computational setup. In particular, we employ a gauge condition that explicitly depends on the dynamical scalar field, going beyond a mere gauge-parameter deformation of the standard linear covariant gauges commonly used in the literature. Exploring different field parameterizations, we identify fixed points belonging to the Gaussian Matter class in four spacetime dimensions that are consistent with previous studies. The real part of the critical exponents associated with the scalar-field mass and the non-minimal scalar-curvature coupling turn out to have very small absolute value. We provide a comprehensive exploration of how the anomalous dimension of the scalar field and field parameterization impact those critical exponents showing that fixed-point candidates with two or four relevant directions emerge.

</details>


### [123] [The three- and four-loop equal-mass sunset integrals and local Calabi--Yau fourfolds and fivefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10821)
*Hartmut Maennel,Pierre Vanhove*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过局部Calabi-Yau四维和五维流形的零亏格Gromov-Witten理论,计算了大欧几里得动量下等质量二维三圈和四圈日落费曼积分,揭示了高圈费曼积分与代数几何中模空间理论的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二圈日落积分由局部Calabi-Yau三维流形的预势支配,但对于L≥3的情况,未标记稳定映射的模空间具有正虚维数且不存在预势。因此需要发展新的数学框架来理解三圈及更高圈的费曼积分结构,探索其与Gromov-Witten理论、重力后代以及边界项的关系。

Method: 采用Gromov-Witten理论的单点势函数方法,利用插入γ_L=H^(L-1)/(L-1)!的单点势F_{γ_L}、其重力后代以及来自Gamma类的ζ(3)边界项来表述费曼积分。通过Frobenius展开分析镜像坐标R中R^(L-1)的系数,利用零亏格递归关系、Picard-Fuchs方程、Mellin变换的极点部分推导解析归一化,并证明任意整插入的多重覆盖变换的整性以及量子K公式。

Result: 在三圈情况下,整个费曼积分可完全用F_{γ_3}显式表示,零亏格递归关系从中确定所有后代级数。四圈情况需要两个对角级数,且ζ(3)项本身由θ_Q F_{γ_4}修正。计算得到了欧几里得轴上的端点值、Novikov变量Q中Taylor半径的界、四圈端点非解析性的计算机辅助证明,以及精确的周期连接多项式和两圈、三圈、四圈归一化的比较。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了高圈日落费曼积分与局部Calabi-Yau流形的Gromov-Witten理论之间的精确对应关系,证明了三圈和四圈积分可通过单点势函数及其后代完全刻画。研究展示了代数几何工具在量子场论计算中的强大应用,为理解更高圈费曼积分的数学结构提供了系统的理论框架和计算方法。

Abstract: We determine the equal-mass two-dimensional three- and four-loop sunset Feynman integrals $I^{(3)}_\circleddash$ and $I^{(4)}_\circleddash$ at large Euclidean momentum in terms of the genus-zero Gromov--Witten theory of the local Calabi--Yau fourfold and fivefold $X_L=\operatorname{Tot}(K_{F_L})$, where $F_L\subset(\mathbb P^1)^{L+1}$, $L=3,4$, is a smooth hypersurface of multidegree $(1,\ldots,1)$. At two loops the sunset integral is governed by the prepotential of a local Calabi--Yau threefold. For $L\geq3$ the moduli spaces of unmarked stable maps have positive virtual dimension and there is no prepotential, therefore the Feynman integral is governed instead by the one-point potential $\mathcal F_{γ_L}$ with insertion $γ_L=H^{L-1}/(L-1)!$, by its gravitational descendants, and by $ζ(3)$ boundary terms coming from the Gamma class. At both loop orders the coefficient of $R^{L-1}$ in the Frobenius expansion in the mirror coordinate $R$ is $-θ_Q\mathcal F_{γ_L}$ with the Novikov variable $Q=\exp(R)$. At three loops the whole Feynman integral is an explicit expression in $\mathcal F_{γ_3}$ alone, because genus-zero recursion determines all descendant series from it; at four loops two diagonal series suffice, and the $ζ(3)$ term is itself corrected by $θ_Q\mathcal F_{γ_4}$. At three loops we also derive the analytic normalization independently, from the Picard--Fuchs equation and the polar part of a Mellin transform, prove integrality of the multiple-cover transforms for arbitrary integral insertions together with an explicit quantum-$K$ formula, and compute the exact period connection polynomials. Finally we give endpoint values along the Euclidean axis, bounds on the Taylor radii in the Novikov variable $Q$, a computer-assisted proof of the four-loop endpoint nonanalyticity, and a comparison of the normalizations at two, three and four loops.

</details>


### [124] [Massive de Sitter Correlators as Finite Mellin-Barnes Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10921)
*Amara McCune*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用Mellin-Barnes表示方法简化了宇宙学对撞机中的重粒子交换过程计算,实现了树图级三重交换双谱和完整单圈三角图的精确求解,为任意质量下多重交换和圈图计算提供了系统方法,用于预测暴胀期间重粒子的振荡特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学对撞机物理中,重粒子在暴胀期间产生的振荡特征信号是探测早期宇宙高能物理的重要窗口。然而,传统计算方法在处理多重粒子交换和圈图修正时面临技术困难,特别是在一般质量情况下缺乏有效的计算框架。需要发展新的数学工具来系统地处理树图级多重交换和圈图级贡献,以获得任意质量下的完整预测。

Method: 采用de Sitter传播子的Mellin-Barnes积分表示作为核心技术工具。对于树图级重粒子交换过程,将其约化为在质量无关的积分轮廓上收敛的有限积分,这些积分在物理运动学条件下自然收敛。对于单圈图,将积分表示为相同的被积函数乘以平直空间的动量因子。利用这一表示方法,在不需要解析延拓的情况下直接计算三重交换双谱,并在一般质量条件下完整求解单圈三角图。

Result: 成功计算了树图级三重交换过程的双谱函数,避免了传统方法中复杂的解析延拓步骤。完整求解了一般质量情况下的单圈三角图贡献。建立了适用于任意质量参数的多重交换和圈图计算框架,能够系统地给出宇宙学对撞机的理论预测。这些结果涵盖了从树图到单圈、从单次交换到多重交换的完整计算体系。

Conclusion: 通过引入Mellin-Barnes表示方法,本文为宇宙学对撞机物理建立了系统的计算框架,实现了任意质量下多重粒子交换和圈图修正的精确计算。该方法避免了解析延拓等技术难点,使得复杂过程的计算变得可行和系统化。这为从宇宙学观测中探测暴胀期间重粒子的振荡特征信号提供了完整的理论预测工具,对早期宇宙高能物理研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Using the Mellin-Barnes representation of de Sitter propagators, we reduce tree-level heavy-particle exchange to finite integrals that converge at physical kinematics on mass-independent contours, and one-loop diagrams to the same integrand times a flat-space momentum factor. We evaluate the triple-exchange bispectrum without analytic continuation and compute the complete one-loop triangle at general mass. These give cosmological collider predictions, the oscillatory signatures of heavy particles during inflation, for multiple exchanges and loops at arbitrary masses.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum geometry at arbitrary genus - I: Anharmonic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10940)
*Mustafa Türe,Mithat Ünsal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过复代数几何与精确WKB方法相结合,解决了高亏格势的微扰/非微扰(P/NP)关系这一长期悬而未决的问题,建立了适用于任意亏格势的全阶量子P/NP关系,并给出了亏格-2和亏格-3非调和振子的显式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在亏格-1势的量子力学中,拓扑平凡鞍点的微扰理论与非微扰瞬子鞍点通过P/NP关系建立了构造性联系。但这种关系能否推广到高亏格势一直是个未解决的问题。核心障碍在于当亏格g≥2时,WKB活跃闭链与Picard-Fuchs基底之间的线性变换属于SL(2g,ℤ)但不属于辛群Sp(2g,ℤ),与亏格-1情形(两群重合)形成对比,这种不匹配阻碍了显式高亏格P/NP关系的建立。

Method: 研究采用复代数几何工具结合精确WKB方法。经典能量守恒关系p²=2(E-V(q))定义了亏格-g黎曼曲面X,其周期通过Abel-Jacobi映射由Jacobi簇J(X)=ℂᵍ/Λ自然组织,同时也由相关Picard-Fuchs方程的解基底独立组织。关键创新是构造了一个修正的黎曼双线性恒等式,该恒等式基于适当变换的交叉矩阵构建,使得WKB活跃周期能够精确满足此恒等式,从而绕过了群论不匹配的障碍。

Result: 成功导出了适用于任意亏格势的全阶量子P/NP关系。针对亏格-2势(五次和六次势)以及亏格-3势(七次和八次势)的非调和振子,给出了该关系的显式表达式。修正的黎曼双线性恒等式完美地协调了WKB活跃闭链与Picard-Fuchs基底之间的SL(2g,ℤ)变换,解决了高亏格情形的本质困难。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将亏格-1势中的P/NP关系推广到任意高亏格情形,解决了量子力学中一个长期存在的理论难题。核心贡献在于识别出高亏格情形下群论结构的本质差异(SL与Sp群的不重合),并通过构造修正的黎曼双线性恒等式巧妙地化解了这一障碍。该结果为理解高亏格势量子系统中微扰与非微扰效应的深层联系提供了完整的数学框架,对量子场论和弦理论中的相关问题具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: In quantum mechanics of genus-1 potentials, perturbation theory around the topologically trivial saddle and non-perturbative instanton saddle are constructively related via the P/NP relation. Whether such a relation persists for higher-genus potentials has remained an open problem. In this work, we resolve it using tools from complex algebraic geometry combined with exact WKB. The classical energy conservation relation $p^2 = 2(E-V(q))$ defines a genus-g Riemann surface $X$, whose periods are naturally organized by its Jacobian variety $J(X) = \mathbb{C}^{\text{g}}/Λ$ via the Abel-Jacobi map, and independently by the solution basis of the associated Picard-Fuchs equations. We show that the physically relevant, WKB-active cycles are generally related to the Picard-Fuchs basis by a linear transformation that lies in $\text{SL}(2\text{g},\mathbb{Z})$ but not in the symplectic group $\text{Sp}(2\text{g},\mathbb{Z})$ once $\text{g}\geq2$, in contrast to the genus-1 case, where the two groups coincide. This mismatch is the obstruction that has made an explicit higher-genus P/NP relation elusive. We show that it can be resolved by passing to a modified Riemann bilinear identity, built from a suitably transformed intersection matrix, which the WKB-active periods do satisfy exactly. The result is an all-orders quantum P/NP relation valid for potentials of arbitrary genus, which we derive explicitly for genus-2 (quintic and sextic) and genus-3 (septic and octic) anharmonic oscillators.

</details>


### [126] [Fermionic Spectral Functions in a Two-Current Gubser-Rocha Model with Axion Momentum Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11323)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用双电流Gubser-Rocha全息模型,引入线性轴子场实现动量弛豫;系统考察三种费米子耦合通道:偶极耦合$p_1$、轴子依赖的张量耦合$p_2$(通过电流混合调制)和矢量耦合$p_3$;通过数值求解引力对偶理论中的费米子方程,计算不同参数(动量弛豫强度$β/μ$、掺杂参数$χ$)下的谱函数,分析零能态、能隙结构、谱权重分布和动量空间不对称性。

Result: $p_1$偶极耦合产生软不对称谱重构,但未形成清晰的硬莫特能隙;$p_2$张量通道显著抑制零能费米峰,将主导谱权重转移至有限频率,形成平底重构结构;$p_3$矢量通道在动量空间产生强谱不对称性,生成开放的类费米弧片段;增大动量弛豫强度$β/μ$普遍展宽$p_2$响应,而$p_3$的单侧特征随背景参数变化呈现非单调行为;不同通道对双电流背景表现出通道依赖的响应模式。

Conclusion: 本研究系统阐明了在全息框架下,电流混合、轴子诱导的动量弛豫与非最小费米子耦合三者如何协同作用重塑费米子谱函数。不同耦合通道产生截然不同的谱特征:从软能隙到平底重构再到费米弧结构,这些结果为理解强关联系统中的非费米液体行为、赝能隙现象和动量空间各向异性提供了全息视角,并揭示了通道选择和动量弛豫机制在决定准粒子性质中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate fermionic spectral functions in a two-current Gubser-Rocha background with linear-axion momentum relaxation. Serving as a comparison channel, the dipole coupling $p_1$ is found to produce a soft asymmetric reconstruction without opening a clean hard Mott gap. Our main analysis then focuses on two axion-dependent non-minimal couplings modulated by current mixing. The $p_2$ tensor channel suppresses the zero-energy Fermi peak and shifts the dominant spectral weight to finite frequencies, resulting in a flat-bottom reconstruction. In contrast, the $p_3$ vector channel creates a strong spectral asymmetry in momentum space, generating open, Fermi-arc-like segments. Varying the momentum-relaxation strength $β/μ$ and the doping parameter $χ$ reveals a channel-dependent response to the two-current background. Increasing $β/μ$ generally broadens the $p_2$ response, whereas the one-sided $p_3$ feature varies non-monotonically under changes of the background parameters. These results clarify how current mixing, axion-induced momentum relaxation, and non-minimal fermion couplings jointly reshape holographic fermionic excitations. These results clarify how current mixing, axion-induced momentum relaxation, and non-minimal fermion couplings jointly reshape holographic fermionic excitations.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Gravity in Asymptotically Flat Spacetimes from Unstable D-Branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11493)
*Roji Pius*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用Witten三次开弦场论(OSFT)在渐近平坦时空中对量子引力进行非微扰表述的方法,通过将闭弦激发映射到OSFT规范不变算符,证明闭弦理论和量子引力可从开弦快子真空中动力学涌现,建立了平坦空间量子引力的可计算非微扰框架。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 该研究采用Witten三次开弦场论作为核心工具,将闭弦谱中的无质量自旋2态(量子引力自由度)映射为OSFT中的规范不变算符。通过在快子真空附近微扰展开计算OSFT关联函数,系统性地再现闭弦S矩阵的全阶亏格展开。这一映射建立了on-shell闭弦激发与开弦场论算符之间的直接对应关系,构造了平坦空间的AdS/CFT对偶类比。

Result: 研究成功证明了闭弦理论可以从开弦快子真空中动力学涌现。通过OSFT关联函数的微扰计算,系统性地重现了相关态的闭弦S矩阵的所有阶数亏格展开,验证了开弦场论与闭弦理论之间的精确对应关系。这一结果确立了开弦场论描述量子引力的有效性和完备性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Witten开弦场论作为平坦空间量子引力完全可计算、非微扰框架的理论地位。通过证明闭弦理论(包括量子引力)从开弦快子真空的动力学涌现,提供了渐近平坦时空中量子引力的具体实现方案,为平坦时空的全息原理提供了可操作的理论范例,推进了弦论在量子引力非微扰研究中的应用。

Abstract: We present a non-perturbative formulation of quantum gravity in asymptotically flat spacetimes using Witten's cubic open string field theory (OSFT) on unstable D-branes. Given that the massless spin-2 sector of the closed string spectrum inherently encodes the degrees of freedom of quantum gravity, we establish this connection by mapping on-shell closed string excitations directly to gauge-invariant operators in OSFT, realizing a concrete flat-space analogue of the AdS/CFT correspondence. By evaluating the OSFT correlation functions of these operators perturbatively around the tachyon vacuum, we systematically reproduce the all-orders genus expansion of the closed string S-matrix for the associated states. This demonstrates that closed string theory-and thus quantum gravity-emerges dynamically from the open string tachyon vacuum, establishing Witten's OSFT as a fully calculable, non-perturbative framework for flat-space quantum gravity.

</details>

Abstract: We propose an analogue of the no particle production condition in two-dimensional anti-de Sitter space, and demonstrate how this criterion uniquely reconstructs the curved-space Liouville and Toda theories via general-multiplicity holographic correlators.
